### ~~DR-DOC-011 — Local source identity ledger prevents duplicate linked-document imports~~ [DISCARDED → see DR-DOC-012]

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | data |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | discarded |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, local-import, source-identity, deduplication, sqlite, link-graph, path-policy, redaction, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-link-import/linked_import_research.json |

#### Requirement

시스템은 로컬 source-path import와 linked-document import가 같은 원본 문서를 여러 번 저장하지 않도록 source identity와 import graph 원장을 SQLite에 유지해야 한다. 원장은 stable `documentId`, source root fingerprint, source-relative path key, canonical realpath hash, content hash, import job state, link edge status를 분리해 저장하고, raw absolute path는 internal-only 값으로 처리해야 한다.

#### Rationale

절대경로만으로 중복을 판단하면 root 이동, symlink 또는 junction alias, 동일 content 복사본, 경로 대소문자 차이, renamed root를 안정적으로 처리할 수 없다. 반대로 content hash만 쓰면 서로 다른 문서의 동일 본문을 잘못 병합할 수 있다. 중복 방지와 cycle 방지는 canonical path 기반 내부 판단과 stable document identity를 함께 사용해야 한다.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: SQLite schema는 local import source identity를 표현하기 위해 source_id, root_fingerprint, source_relative_path, path_key, canonical_path_internal 또는 canonical_path_hash, content_hash, documentId, source_mtime_or_revision, first_seen_at, last_seen_at, last_import_job_id, import_state를 저장한다. raw absolute path와 canonical path 원문은 internal-only field로 취급한다.
- [ ] AC-2: Primary document identity는 stable `documentId`이고, local source duplicate/update lookup은 `(source_id, path_key)`와 canonical path hash를 함께 사용한다. `content_hash`는 no-op update, stale job, vector reuse, move/copy 후보 판단에 사용하며 단독 primary identity로 사용하지 않는다.
- [ ] AC-3: Import traversal의 visited set은 canonical realpath hash 또는 canonical source identity를 사용해 same-run cycle과 symlink alias loop를 차단하고, persistent duplicate check는 source root fingerprint와 source-relative path key를 사용해 run 간 중복 저장을 막는다.
- [ ] AC-4: 동일 `(source_id, path_key)`가 이미 active이면 importer는 기존 `documentId`를 재사용한다. content hash가 같으면 write를 skip하고, content hash가 다르면 같은 `documentId` revision update와 background indexing enqueue로 처리한다.
- [ ] AC-5: 새 path가 발견되었고 이전 path가 missing/deleted이며 content hash와 optional inode/file-id evidence가 같은 경우 move candidate로 기록하고 같은 `documentId` 보존 정책을 적용할 수 있다. 이전 path가 active이면 같은 content hash라도 copy/duplicate candidate로 기록하고 자동 병합하지 않는다.
- [ ] AC-6: Import graph ledger는 `import_jobs`, `import_job_documents`, `import_job_edges` 또는 동등한 tables로 root document, parent source identity, target source identity, depth, original href, normalized href, anchor, edge status enum resolved|missing|external|skipped_cycle|skipped_depth|skipped_limit|path_policy_violation|ambiguous, linked `documentId`를 추적한다.
- [ ] AC-7: Path policy failures, missing files, excluded files, and external links are retained as edge diagnostics for graph/search diagnostics but do not create document rows or duplicate files in the knowledge-store root.
- [ ] AC-8: All external responses, logs, diagnostics export, backup manifest, and smart_search traces expose only `documentId`, source-relative path, redacted path token, status counts, and diagnostic codes. They must not expose raw local absolute path, canonical path, app userData index path, or credential-bearing provider URL.
- [ ] AC-9: Schema fixture verifies Windows drive paths, UNC paths, POSIX paths, case-insensitive Windows collision, symlink/junction alias, root fingerprint change, duplicate basename, identical content in different active paths, moved path candidate, and Korean/space path normalization.

#### Verification Evidence

| Evidence ID | Type | Reference | Covers | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

#### Trace Links

| Type | Reference | Relation | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Requirement | FR-DOC-029 | depends_on | Functional linked-document import requires duplicate and cycle-safe source identity ledger. |
| Requirement | DR-DOC-006 | extends | Extends SQLite documents/sources/links/jobs schema with local source identity and import graph details. |
| Requirement | DR-DOC-009 | depends_on | Uses source root fingerprint and scanner containment policy. |
| Requirement | SEC-DOC-003 | depends_on | Source identity and import graph diagnostics must share deterministic redaction policy. |
| Code | src/main/search-sqlite-store.js:L52-L80 | compatibility_reference | Current Wave 1.5 keyword cache uses file_path as primary key and lacks Wave 2 source identity/import graph ledger. |
| Code | src/main/search-sqlite-store.js:L314-L319 | compatibility_reference | Current sourceRoot normalization uses path.resolve/lowercase only; Wave 2 needs root fingerprint and realpath/canonical identity handling. |
| Code | src/main/mcp-save.js:L123-L128 | addition_site | Current save path writes timestamped destination without duplicate source identity lookup. |
| Research | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md:L127-L168 | derived_from | Existing research schema includes sources/documents path identity fields; linked import adds origin identity and import graph constraints. |

#### Research / Analysis

- Sub-agent duplicate identity review concluded raw absolute path alone is not a suitable primary identity.
- Recommended identity model: stable documentId for public identity, `(source_id, path_key)` for update lookup, root_fingerprint/source-relative path for source identity, canonical realpath hash for visited/cycle detection, content_hash for no-op/stale/move-copy candidate handling.
- This requirement keeps absolute/canonical paths internal and aligns linked import with SEC-DOC-003 redaction.

### ~~CON-DOC-001 — Linked local Markdown import is duplicate-safe user-confirmed and MCP-inert~~ [DISCARDED → see CON-DOC-002]

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | constraint |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | discarded |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, local-import, deduplication, source-identity, mcp-inert, path-policy, sqlite, redaction, concurrency, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-linked-import-dedup/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 linked local Markdown import를 사용자가 명시적으로 승인한 Settings/local import workflow로만 실행해야 하며, 어떤 MCP 도구도 이 import를 직접 또는 암묵적으로 시작하지 않아야 한다. Importer는 source identity lookup, SQLite uniqueness/upsert, destination containment, path redaction, and encoded traversal rejection을 함께 적용해 이미 저장된 문서를 재저장하지 않고, 동시 실행이나 path alias가 있어도 하나의 canonical source에 하나의 active documentId와 저장본만 유지해야 한다.

#### Rationale

FR-DOC-029와 DR-DOC-011은 linked import와 source identity ledger의 핵심을 이미 정의하지만, 보안 리뷰에서는 open_markdown/update_markdown filePath 경로를 통한 MCP 우회, symlink TOCTOU, concurrent import race, destination collision, encoded traversal fixture가 별도 제약으로 명시되면 구현자가 더 안전하게 닫을 수 있다고 보았다. 이 요구사항은 새 기능을 추가하지 않고 기존 linked import 요구사항의 cross-surface safety invariant를 release-gating 제약으로 묶는다.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: AC-1: Linked local Markdown import는 Settings/import dialog 또는 source-path-backed local import action에서 사용자가 import root와 실행을 명시적으로 승인한 경우에만 시작된다. `open_markdown`, `update_markdown`, `save_document`, `search_documents`, `search_projects`, `smart_search`를 포함한 어떤 MCP tool call도 linked import, recursive import, bulk source scan, indexing rebuild, clear, retry, cancel, model change를 직접 또는 암묵적으로 시작하지 않는다.
- [ ] AC-2: AC-2: Importer는 승인 시점의 import root realpath 또는 equivalent canonical identity와 root_fingerprint를 캡처하고, 각 candidate read/copy 직전에도 lexical containment와 canonical realpath containment를 재검증한다. TOCTOU로 symlink, junction, hard-link escape, root replacement, outside-root target이 감지되면 document row나 destination write를 만들지 않고 edge diagnostic으로 기록한다.
- [ ] AC-3: AC-3: Same-run traversal은 active recursion stack과 visited set에 canonical realpath hash 또는 canonical source identity를 사용하며, raw absolute path, destination path, content_hash, inode/file-id, documentId만으로 cycle 또는 duplicate 여부를 결정하지 않는다.
- [ ] AC-4: AC-4: Persistent duplicate/update lookup은 SQLite unique constraint 또는 equivalent transaction boundary로 `(source_id, path_key)`와 canonical path hash alias를 원자적으로 upsert한다. 동일 canonical source에 대해 concurrent import jobs가 겹쳐도 하나의 active `documentId`와 하나의 destination write만 성공해야 하며, loser job은 existing/skipped_duplicate 또는 equivalent result로 수렴한다.
- [ ] AC-5: AC-5: 이미 active인 `(source_id, path_key)` 또는 unique canonical path hash alias가 있으면 importer는 기존 `documentId`를 재사용하고 새 파일을 쓰지 않는다. content hash가 같으면 duplicate write와 duplicate indexing job을 skip하고, content hash가 다르면 같은 `documentId` revision update와 common post-save indexing enqueue로 처리한다.
- [ ] AC-6: AC-6: 이전 path가 inactive/missing/deleted인 move candidate에서 `documentId`를 보존하려면 content_hash만으로는 충분하지 않다. importer는 matching file-id/inode 또는 equivalent platform evidence가 있거나 사용자가 명시적으로 승인한 경우에만 move로 채택하며, 그렇지 않으면 ambiguous diagnostic으로 남기고 자동 병합하지 않는다.
- [ ] AC-7: AC-7: Destination path는 source identity 결정을 마친 뒤 derived value로만 계산된다. importer는 source-relative layout 또는 deterministic map을 사용할 때도 absolute segment, drive/UNC segment, `..`, empty/NUL-like segment, encoded slash/backslash/traversal, reserved device name, case/Unicode normalization collision을 거부하거나 deterministic conflict name으로 격리하고, final destination realpath containment를 검증한다.
- [ ] AC-8: AC-8: 기존 destination file 또는 legacy saved document가 있는데 source identity ledger row가 없으면 importer는 content_hash, frontmatter identity, optional file-id/inode, and destination containment evidence가 모두 일관될 때만 adoption candidate로 기록한다. 증거가 불충분하거나 여러 후보가 있으면 overwrite/merge하지 않고 ambiguous diagnostic을 반환한다.
- [ ] AC-9: AC-9: Local import result, diagnostics, logs, backup/export manifest, smart_search trace는 raw local absolute path, canonical path, app userData index path, source root path, destination path, provider URL credential을 노출하지 않고 `documentId`, source-relative path, redacted path token, status counts, diagnostic code만 노출한다.
- [ ] AC-10: AC-10: Release-gating fixture는 root document already imported, linked child already imported, concurrent duplicate import, symlink/junction alias, case-insensitive alias, root fingerprint change, destination collision, legacy destination without source identity, `%2e%2e`, `%2f`, `%5c`, double-encoded traversal, NUL-like input, Windows reserved names, Unicode normalization collision, Korean/space filename을 포함하고 canonical source당 active 저장본이 최대 하나임을 검증한다.

#### Verification Evidence

| Evidence ID | Type | Reference | Covers | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

#### Trace Links

| Type | Reference | Relation | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Requirement | FR-DOC-029 | depends_on | Functional linked-document import behavior supplies the user-confirmed import workflow and duplicate-save outcome. |
| Requirement | DR-DOC-011 | depends_on | Source identity ledger supplies documentId, source_id/path_key, canonical path hash, content hash, and import graph persistence. |
| Requirement | SEC-DOC-003 | depends_on | Import diagnostics/logs/search traces use the shared deterministic redaction policy. |
| Requirement | IR-MCP-018 | depends_on | MCP save/search contract remains strict and no linked import control fields are added. |
| Requirement | CON-MCP-007 | depends_on | Eight-tool conformance must continue to reject indexing/import control tools and fields. |
| Code | src/main/link-parser.js:L206-L224 | compatibility_reference | Current parser decodes href and resolves local paths; encoded traversal fixtures must harden this behavior for import. |
| Code | src/main/mcp-save.js:L67-L128 | negative | Current save path writes timestamped single files and does not provide duplicate-safe linked import or source identity lookup. |
| Code | src/main/search-sqlite-store.js:L52-L80 | negative | Current Wave 1.5 keyword cache uses file_path as primary key and lacks source identity/import graph uniqueness. |
| Code | src/main/search-sqlite-store.js:L314-L319 | negative | Current source root normalization is path.resolve/lowercase only and lacks realpath/root fingerprint identity. |
| Test | test/test-link-extraction.js:L10-L87 | compatibility_reference | Existing extraction fixtures are reusable but need encoded traversal and import-dedup cases. |
| Test | test/test-link-tree-build.js:L35-L61 | compatibility_reference | Existing tree traversal cycle tests are reusable but not sufficient for canonical source saved-once behavior. |

#### Research / Analysis

- Five sub-agent review concluded the existing FR-DOC-029/DR-DOC-011 direction is correct and absolute path alone must not be used as primary identity. The added constraint closes MCP bypass, TOCTOU, concurrent import, destination collision, legacy adoption, and encoded traversal gaps without adding MCP tools or broad reindexing controls.

#### Implementation Notes

- Implement as an internal local import service layered over the Wave 2 SQLite source identity ledger. Treat absolute paths as internal inputs for canonicalization/containment only. Prefer transactionally enforced unique source identity rows over best-effort in-memory duplicate checks.

| Date | Change | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-07-01 | Created | add-requirement |

### ~~FR-DOC-030 — Existing knowledge-store Markdown files are adopted into indexing without rewrite and keep broken-link diagnostics~~ [DISCARDED → see FR-DOC-032]

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | functional |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | discarded |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, legacy-adoption, knowledge-store, background-indexing, link-graph, broken-links, source-identity, redaction, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-linked-import-dedup/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 구성된 knowledge-store root 안에 이미 존재하지만 MCP save_document, viewer save, local linked import로 등록된 source identity가 없는 Markdown 파일도 기존 파일을 다시 쓰거나 복사하지 않고 smart indexing 대상으로 채택해야 한다. 채택된 legacy document는 stable documentId, knowledge-store source identity, source-relative path_key, content hash, link edge diagnostics를 SQLite 원장에 기록하고, broken link는 실패나 삭제가 아니라 검색과 진단에 사용할 수 있는 missing 또는 equivalent edge status로 보존해야 한다.

#### Rationale

사용자는 DocuLightViewer를 거치지 않고 지식 저장소 폴더에 Markdown 파일을 직접 넣을 수 있다. 이 경우 원본 local source identity는 없지만 문서는 이미 knowledge-store에 존재하므로 인덱싱되어야 한다. 반대로 이 파일을 linked local import 원본과 자동 병합하거나 다시 저장하면 중복 저장, 오판 병합, path leak, link graph 누락이 발생한다. Broken link도 인덱싱 실패가 아니라 graph diagnostic으로 보존해야 나중에 대상 문서가 추가되었을 때 재해석할 수 있다.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: Settings indexing startup, source-root scan, or equivalent background indexing path는 configured knowledge-store root 안의 `.md`와 `.markdown` 파일 중 source identity/import ledger row가 없는 기존 파일을 legacy knowledge-store document로 채택할 수 있다. 이 동작은 MCP search 호출이나 MCP import control tool로 시작되지 않는다.
- [ ] AC-2: Legacy adoption은 기존 파일을 rewrite, copy, move, rename하지 않고 SQLite documents/source identity ledger에 `source_kind=knowledge_store` 또는 equivalent marker, stable `documentId`, source-relative path, normalized `path_key`, content_hash, mtime/size 또는 equivalent freshness evidence, path_status=active를 기록한다.
- [ ] AC-3: Legacy file 안에 trusted DocuLight documentId frontmatter 또는 equivalent stable id가 있고 SQLite uniqueness/redaction policy를 통과하면 재사용할 수 있다. 없거나 충돌하면 importer는 ledger insert에서 stable documentId를 생성하며, documentId 생성을 위해 파일 본문을 수정하지 않는다.
- [ ] AC-4: Legacy adoption은 local source-path import identity와 자동 병합하지 않는다. 같은 content_hash, 같은 basename, 같은 title/docName만으로 linked import source와 같은 문서라고 판단하지 않으며, CON-DOC-002의 evidence 또는 사용자 승인 없이 overwrite, merge, destination replacement를 수행하지 않는다.
- [ ] AC-5: Legacy adopted document는 `requested_by=knowledge_store.legacy_adoption` 또는 equivalent value로 common post-save indexing entrypoint에 enqueue되어 metadata extraction, category/documentTags classification, persistent link graph, chunking, keyword FTS, optional embedding, HNSW update lifecycle을 따른다. 파일 내용이 변하지 않은 재스캔은 duplicate write와 duplicate indexing job을 만들지 않고 freshness만 갱신한다.
- [ ] AC-6: Legacy adopted document의 link extraction은 기존 parser-compatible Markdown inline links와 wikilinks를 사용하되, target resolution은 같은 knowledge-store source root와 승인된 source records 안에서만 수행한다. target이 active documentId/path_key로 해석되면 edge status는 resolved가 되고, target이 없거나 제외되거나 root 밖으로 나가면 document row를 만들지 않고 missing, external, skipped, path_policy_violation, ambiguous 또는 equivalent status를 기록한다.
- [ ] AC-7: Broken link edge는 indexing failure가 아니며 원본 href, normalized href, target anchor, source documentId, source chunk/line 또는 equivalent location, diagnostic code를 원장에 남긴다. Broken link는 link boost의 resolved-link 점수에는 포함하지 않지만 includeDiagnostics 또는 Settings diagnostics에서 broken/unresolved count로 표시할 수 있다.
- [ ] AC-8: 이전에 missing이던 target 문서가 나중에 저장, legacy-adopt, or local-import되면 다음 indexing reconciliation에서 기존 edge를 resolved로 갱신할 수 있어야 한다. 반대로 target 문서가 삭제되거나 제외되면 documentId tombstone/path_status와 edge status를 missing 또는 stale equivalent로 갱신하되 source document 인덱싱 전체를 실패시키지 않는다.
- [ ] AC-9: Legacy adoption과 broken-link diagnostics의 external response, logs, backup/export manifest, smart_search trace는 raw local absolute path, canonical path, knowledge-store root path, app userData path, destination path를 노출하지 않고 documentId, source-relative path, redacted path token, edge status counts, diagnostic code만 노출한다.
- [ ] AC-10: Release-gating fixture는 pre-existing knowledge-store root files without ledger rows, trusted documentId frontmatter reuse, documentId collision, duplicate content in two active files, same basename in different directories, broken relative link, broken wikilink, target added later, target removed later, excluded target, outside-root link, symlink/junction escape, Korean/space filename을 포함하고 no rewrite, stable documentId, indexing enqueue, missing/resolved edge reconciliation, redaction을 검증한다.

#### Research / Analysis

- Follow-up review found that duplicate-safe linked import does not by itself guarantee indexing of Markdown files already present in the configured knowledge-store root but missing source identity rows. This requirement treats those files as existing knowledge-store documents: index them without rewriting, keep source identity conservative, and preserve broken links as diagnosable graph edges.

#### Implementation Notes

- Implement legacy adoption as part of Settings/background scanner reconciliation, not MCP tools. Use a knowledge-store source record and generated or trusted documentId, enqueue indexing, and leave local source-path merge decisions ambiguous unless CON-DOC-002 evidence or user approval exists.

### ~~CON-DOC-003 — Broken Markdown link edge lifecycle is canonical non-blocking redacted and MCP-inert~~ [DISCARDED → see DR-DOC-012 +1]

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | constraint |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | discarded |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, link-graph, broken-links, diagnostics, redaction, mcp-inert, smart-search, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-broken-link-policy/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 linked local import, legacy knowledge-store adoption, persistent link graph indexing에서 깨진 Markdown 링크를 인덱싱 실패나 자동 import 신호로 처리하지 않고, canonical edge status와 redacted diagnostics를 가진 non-blocking graph edge로 관리해야 한다. resolved edge만 link ranking과 linkedTo/linkedFrom 필터에 사용하고, broken edge는 문서 저장, source identity 생성, destination write, recursive import, MCP-triggered reconciliation을 만들지 않아야 한다.

#### Rationale

FR-DOC-029, DR-DOC-011, CON-DOC-002, FR-DOC-030은 broken link를 진단 edge로 보존하는 방향을 이미 정의하지만, 상태 enum과 전이 규칙이 흩어져 있어 구현자가 broken edge를 ranking, import, MCP-triggered scan, raw href diagnostics에 잘못 섞을 위험이 있다. 세 개의 read-only sub-agent review는 canonical lifecycle, redaction boundary, search/ranking neutrality를 별도 constraint로 고정해야 한다고 결론냈다.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: Broken-link policy applies uniformly to linked local source-path import, legacy knowledge-store adoption, persistent link graph indexing, Settings diagnostics, export/backup manifests, logs, smart_search diagnostics, and smart_search traces.
- [ ] AC-2: Persistent link graph stores each extracted Markdown inline link or wikilink edge with one canonical status enum: resolved, missing, external, path_policy_violation, skipped, ambiguous, stale. Diagnostic code must disambiguate skipped and path policy reasons such as depth limit, excluded target, cycle, encoded traversal, or containment failure.
- [ ] AC-3: resolved status requires a unique active target documentId or path_key. Only resolved edges may set to_document_id, contribute linkScore, reciprocal link, or same-cluster boost, or satisfy linkedTo and linkedFrom filters.
- [ ] AC-4: missing, external, skipped, ambiguous, and stale edges are non-blocking diagnostics. They must not create document rows, source identity rows, destination files, recursive import jobs, or resolved-link ranking boost, and they must not cause the source document indexing job to fail when the source document itself is readable.
- [ ] AC-5: path_policy_violation is used for outside-root targets, lexical traversal, encoded slash or backslash traversal, NUL-like input, drive, UNC, or absolute path segments, root replacement, symlink or junction escape, or failed canonical realpath containment. Such edges must not create document rows, destination writes, source identity rows, or import jobs.
- [ ] AC-6: Target resolution performs lexical containment before filesystem probing and canonical realpath containment before read, copy, or write. If resolution yields multiple active candidates by documentId, path_key, docName, basename, case folding, or Unicode normalization, the edge status is ambiguous and the system must not auto-merge, overwrite, or pick a winner without unique stable identity evidence.
- [ ] AC-7: raw href, normalized href, canonical path, source root, destination path, app userData path, and credential-bearing provider values are internal-only. External responses, Settings diagnostics, logs, exports, backup manifests, and smart_search traces may expose only documentId, source-relative source path, redacted href or path token, edge status, diagnostic code, and aggregate counts.
- [ ] AC-8: BM25, FTS, and smart_search keyword text must not be polluted with synthetic diagnostic tokens such as broken or missing. Authored link text or href may remain searchable as normal document content, but broken-edge metadata is exposed only through link graph diagnostics.
- [ ] AC-9: Reconciliation transitions missing, ambiguous, or stale edges to resolved only when a later save_document, viewer save, legacy adoption, or local import produces a unique active target. Target deletion, exclusion, tombstone, or unconfirmed move transitions a previously resolved edge to stale or missing without failing source document indexing.
- [ ] AC-10: MCP open_markdown, update_markdown, save_document, search_documents, search_projects, and smart_search must not treat Markdown links or MCP-supplied path-like fields as approved import roots and must not trigger recursive import, bulk scan, rebuild, retry, cancel, or broken-link reconciliation. smart_search includeDiagnostics may expose redacted aggregate link status counts, link graph readiness, and last reconciliation or index revision, while linkedTo and linkedFrom remain resolved-document filters only.
- [ ] AC-11: Release-gating fixtures cover broken relative link, broken wikilink, external URL, anchor-only link, excluded target, non-Markdown target, outside-root relative link, Windows absolute path, UNC path, POSIX absolute path, symlink or junction escape, encoded traversal variants, ambiguous basename, case, and Unicode collision, target added later, target removed later, Korean and space filename, smart_search includeDiagnostics redaction, and absence of indexing failure for readable source documents.

#### Research / Analysis

- Three read-only sub-agent reviews found that the current SRS direction is correct but needs a canonical edge lifecycle, no-ranking/no-filter rule for unresolved edges, redacted href/path diagnostics, and MCP non-expansion for reconciliation/import behavior.

#### Implementation Notes

- Implement as a link graph indexing and reconciliation invariant shared by local import, legacy adoption, Settings diagnostics, and smart_search diagnostics. Keep raw href/path values internal and use existing parser-compatible extraction only after containment and redaction boundaries are defined.

### ~~DR-DOC-012 — Local source identity ledger uses canonical link edge lifecycle diagnostics~~ [DISCARDED → see DR-DOC-013]

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | data |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | discarded |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, local-import, source-identity, deduplication, sqlite, link-graph, broken-links, path-policy, redaction, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-link-import/linked_import_research.json, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-broken-link-policy/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 로컬 source-path import와 linked-document import가 같은 원본 문서를 여러 번 저장하지 않도록 source identity와 import graph 원장을 SQLite에 유지해야 하며, link edge 상태는 canonical lifecycle enum과 diagnostic code 조합으로 저장해야 한다. 원장은 stable documentId, source root fingerprint, source-relative path key, canonical realpath hash, content hash, import job state, canonical edge status, diagnostic code를 분리해 저장하고 raw absolute path와 raw href는 internal-only 값으로 처리해야 한다.

#### Rationale

Supersedes DR-DOC-011 because the earlier import graph ledger used skipped_cycle, skipped_depth, skipped_limit as separate edge statuses, while the broken-link policy now requires one canonical skipped status plus diagnostic codes. The replacement preserves source identity and duplicate prevention behavior while aligning the data model with the canonical broken-link lifecycle in CON-DOC-004.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: SQLite schema represents local import source identity with source_id, root_fingerprint, source_relative_path, path_key, canonical_path_internal or canonical_path_hash, content_hash, documentId, source_mtime_or_revision, first_seen_at, last_seen_at, last_import_job_id, and import_state. Raw absolute path and canonical path source values are internal-only fields.
- [ ] AC-2: Primary document identity is stable documentId, and local source duplicate or update lookup uses source_id plus path_key together with canonical path hash alias. content_hash is used for no-op update, stale job, vector reuse, and move or copy candidate handling, but is not the primary identity by itself.
- [ ] AC-3: Import traversal visited set uses canonical realpath hash or canonical source identity to block same-run cycles and symlink alias loops. Persistent duplicate check uses source root fingerprint and source-relative path key to prevent repeated saves across runs.
- [ ] AC-4: If the same active source_id plus path_key already exists, importer reuses the existing documentId. If content hash is unchanged, duplicate write and duplicate indexing job are skipped; if content hash changed, the same documentId receives a revision update and common post-save indexing enqueue.
- [ ] AC-5: If a new path appears while a previous path is inactive, missing, or deleted, importer may preserve documentId only with matching file-id, inode, or equivalent platform evidence, or explicit user approval. If the previous path remains active, identical content is recorded as copy or duplicate candidate and is not auto-merged.
- [ ] AC-6: Import graph ledger tracks root document, parent source identity, target source identity, depth, original href, normalized href, anchor, canonical edge status, diagnostic_code, and linked documentId when resolved. The canonical edge status enum is exactly resolved, missing, external, path_policy_violation, skipped, ambiguous, stale; cycle, depth limit, file count limit, byte limit, excluded target, non-Markdown target, image or binary target, encoded traversal, and containment failure are diagnostic_code values under the appropriate canonical status.
- [ ] AC-7: Path policy failures, missing files, excluded files, external links, skipped traversal limits, ambiguous candidates, and stale targets are retained as edge diagnostics for graph/search diagnostics but do not create document rows, source identity rows, destination files, or duplicate files in the knowledge-store root.
- [ ] AC-8: All external responses, logs, diagnostics export, backup manifest, Settings diagnostics, smart_search diagnostics, and smart_search traces expose only documentId, source-relative path, redacted href or path token, status counts, and diagnostic codes. They must not expose raw local absolute path, canonical path, raw href, normalized href, app userData index path, or credential-bearing provider URL.
- [ ] AC-9: Schema and reconciliation fixtures verify Windows drive paths, UNC paths, POSIX paths, case-insensitive collision, Unicode normalization collision, symlink or junction alias, root fingerprint change, duplicate basename, identical content in different active paths, moved path candidate, broken relative link, broken wikilink, skipped cycle, skipped depth, target added later, target removed later, and Korean or space path normalization.

#### Research / Analysis

- Follow-up evaluator found DR-DOC-011 status enum drift against the canonical broken-link policy. This replacement keeps the source identity ledger semantics while moving skipped_* variants into diagnostic_code values under canonical skipped status.

#### Implementation Notes

- Use canonical edge status for query/filter/ranking semantics and diagnostic_code for reason-level observability. Keep raw href/path data in internal ledger fields only and run all exported diagnostics through the shared redaction policy.

#### Requirement

시스템은 linked local import, legacy knowledge-store adoption, persistent link graph indexing에서 깨진 Markdown 링크를 인덱싱 실패나 자동 import 신호로 처리하지 않고, canonical edge status와 redacted diagnostics를 가진 non-blocking graph edge로 관리해야 한다. resolved edge만 link ranking과 linkedTo/linkedFrom 필터에 사용하고, broken edge는 문서 저장, source identity 생성, destination write, recursive import, MCP-triggered synchronous reconciliation 또는 MCP control surface를 만들지 않아야 한다. 일반 save/adoption/import 후 background indexing reconciliation은 별도 작업으로만 상태를 갱신할 수 있다.

#### Rationale

Supersedes CON-DOC-003 after evaluation found two issues: DR-DOC-011 still carried split skipped_* statuses, and CON-DOC-003 did not clearly distinguish normal post-save background reconciliation from MCP-triggered reconciliation controls. This replacement depends on DR-DOC-012 for the canonical ledger model and states that MCP calls may enqueue ordinary save/indexing work but must not expose or synchronously trigger broken-link reconciliation controls.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: Broken-link policy applies uniformly to linked local source-path import, legacy knowledge-store adoption, persistent link graph indexing, Settings diagnostics, export/backup manifests, logs, smart_search diagnostics, and smart_search traces.
- [ ] AC-2: Persistent link graph stores each extracted Markdown inline link or wikilink edge with the canonical status enum from DR-DOC-012: resolved, missing, external, path_policy_violation, skipped, ambiguous, stale. Reason-level details such as cycle, depth limit, excluded target, non-Markdown target, encoded traversal, or containment failure are diagnostic_code values, not separate edge statuses.
- [ ] AC-3: resolved status requires a unique active target documentId or path_key. Only resolved edges may set to_document_id, contribute linkScore, reciprocal link, or same-cluster boost, or satisfy linkedTo and linkedFrom filters.
- [ ] AC-4: missing, external, skipped, ambiguous, and stale edges are non-blocking diagnostics. They must not create document rows, source identity rows, destination files, recursive import jobs, or resolved-link ranking boost, and they must not cause the source document indexing job to fail when the source document itself is readable.
- [ ] AC-5: path_policy_violation is used for outside-root targets, lexical traversal, encoded slash or backslash traversal, NUL-like input, drive, UNC, or absolute path segments, root replacement, symlink or junction escape, or failed canonical realpath containment. Such edges must not create document rows, destination writes, source identity rows, or import jobs.
- [ ] AC-6: Target resolution performs lexical containment before filesystem probing and canonical realpath containment before read, copy, or write. If resolution yields multiple active candidates by documentId, path_key, docName, basename, case folding, or Unicode normalization, the edge status is ambiguous and the system must not auto-merge, overwrite, or pick a winner without unique stable identity evidence.
- [ ] AC-7: raw href, normalized href, canonical path, source root, destination path, app userData path, and credential-bearing provider values are internal-only. External responses, Settings diagnostics, logs, exports, backup manifests, and smart_search traces may expose only documentId, source-relative source path, redacted href or path token, edge status, diagnostic code, and aggregate counts.
- [ ] AC-8: BM25, FTS, and smart_search keyword text must not be polluted with synthetic diagnostic tokens such as broken or missing. Authored link text or href may remain searchable as normal document content, but broken-edge metadata is exposed only through link graph diagnostics.
- [ ] AC-9: Reconciliation transitions missing, ambiguous, or stale edges to resolved only when a later save_document, viewer save, legacy adoption, or local import has produced a unique active target and the normal background indexing or link graph reconciliation job processes that target. Target deletion, exclusion, tombstone, or unconfirmed move transitions a previously resolved edge to stale or missing without failing source document indexing.
- [ ] AC-10: MCP open_markdown, update_markdown, save_document, search_documents, search_projects, and smart_search must not treat Markdown links or MCP-supplied path-like fields as approved import roots and must not expose or synchronously trigger recursive import, bulk scan, rebuild, retry, cancel, or broken-link reconciliation controls. save_document may write a document and enqueue ordinary post-save indexing, but any broken-link resolution happens later through the background indexing lifecycle and is only observable as current redacted diagnostics.
- [ ] AC-11: smart_search includeDiagnostics may expose redacted aggregate link status counts, link graph readiness, and last reconciliation or index revision. linkedTo and linkedFrom remain resolved-document filters only and must not match missing, external, skipped, ambiguous, stale, or path_policy_violation edges.
- [ ] AC-12: Release-gating fixtures cover broken relative link, broken wikilink, external URL, anchor-only link, excluded target, non-Markdown target, outside-root relative link, Windows absolute path, UNC path, POSIX absolute path, symlink or junction escape, encoded traversal variants, ambiguous basename, case, and Unicode collision, target added later, target removed later, Korean and space filename, smart_search includeDiagnostics redaction, absence of indexing failure for readable source documents, and absence of MCP import/reconciliation controls.

| Requirement | IR-MCP-018 | depends_on | Eight-tool MCP contract exposes smart_search diagnostics without adding import or indexing control tools. |
| Requirement | CON-MCP-007 | depends_on | Transport conformance must continue to reject extra import/indexing/reconciliation tools or fields. |
| Code | src/main/link-parser.js:L175-L230 | compatibility_reference | Current parser extracts local links and wikilinks; Wave 2 link graph policy must preserve extraction compatibility while adding status diagnostics and redaction. |
| Code | src/main/link-parser.js:L243-L305 | compatibility_reference | Current viewer traversal has recursion, visited, depth, and file caps that inform skipped/cycle handling. |
| Code | src/main/search-sqlite-store.js:L52-L80 | negative | Current Wave 1.5 keyword cache lacks persistent link edge status, diagnostic code, and reconciliation lifecycle tables. |
| Test | test/test-link-extraction.js:L10-L78 | compatibility_reference | Existing extraction fixtures can be extended for broken relative links, wikilinks, external links, and encoded/path-policy cases. |

#### Research / Analysis

- Final evaluation required canonical skipped status consistency and a clearer distinction between save_document causing ordinary background indexing and MCP exposing or synchronously triggering broken-link reconciliation controls. This replacement resolves both.

#### Implementation Notes

- Implement as a link graph indexing and reconciliation invariant shared by local import, legacy adoption, Settings diagnostics, and smart_search diagnostics. Keep raw href/path values internal. Treat save_document as a write plus ordinary indexing enqueue only; do not add MCP reconciliation controls.

### ~~FR-DOC-031 — Local linked Markdown import preserves graph connectivity with canonical diagnostics and no duplicate saves~~ [DISCARDED → see FR-DOC-033]

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | functional |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | discarded |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, local-import, linked-documents, knowledge-store, link-graph, deduplication, path-policy, settings-import, broken-links, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-link-import/linked_import_research.json, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-broken-link-policy/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 source-path가 있는 로컬 Markdown 문서를 사용자가 지식 저장소로 가져오도록 명시적으로 실행하면, 해당 문서의 로컬 Markdown 링크와 링크된 문서의 링크를 bounded traversal로 따라가며 지식 저장소에 저장해야 한다. 재귀 import는 무한 루프를 방지하고 이미 저장된 source 문서를 중복 저장하지 않으며, 모든 unresolved 또는 skipped link outcome은 DR-DOC-012의 canonical edge status와 diagnostic_code로 기록되어 background indexing과 smart_search diagnostics에서 사용할 수 있어야 한다.

#### Rationale

Supersedes FR-DOC-029 because the earlier requirement listed skipped_cycle, skipped_depth, and skipped_limit as result names, which can be confused with non-canonical edge statuses. This replacement preserves the linked local import behavior while aligning all skipped and broken outcomes with DR-DOC-012 and CON-DOC-004.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: Linked-document import runs only when the user explicitly starts a source-path-backed local Markdown import or Settings/import dialog workflow. It must not add filePath, sourceFilePath, recursive, forceIndex, rebuild, or equivalent import/indexing control fields to the content-only save_document MCP schema, and it must not add a separate MCP indexing/import control tool.
- [ ] AC-2: Importer deterministically resolves Markdown inline links and wikilinks relative to the root document directory and treats only .md and .markdown local targets as import candidates. Remote URL, protocol URL, anchor-only link, image, binary, and non-Markdown targets are not imported and are recorded as link edge diagnostics using DR-DOC-012 canonical status plus diagnostic_code.
- [ ] AC-3: Traversal uses a deterministic BFS or DFS order, configurable default depth at least 2, max depth, max file count, max total bytes, AbortSignal or equivalent cancel boundary, and a visited canonical source identity set so A->B->C->A, self-link, duplicated edge, symlink alias, and repeated candidate cases terminate without infinite loops.
- [ ] AC-4: Each import candidate is lexically normalized and, when filesystem probing is needed, canonical realpath containment is checked against the configured source root or user-approved import root. Traversal, symlink, junction, hard-link escape, outside-root target, encoded slash/backslash traversal, absolute drive/UNC/POSIX segment, and root replacement produce path_policy_violation diagnostics and no document row or destination write.
- [ ] AC-5: If a stored source identity already exists, importer reuses the existing documentId and does not write a new file. If content hash is unchanged, duplicate write and duplicate indexing job are skipped; if content hash changed, the same documentId receives a revision update and common post-save indexing enqueue.
- [ ] AC-6: Newly saved linked documents stay under the configured knowledge-store root using source-relative layout or a deterministic source-to-destination map that keeps Markdown links resolvable. If link rewrite is performed, the ledger tracks original href, normalized target, rewritten href, canonical edge status, diagnostic_code, and target documentId when resolved.
- [ ] AC-7: Import result reports imported, updated, existing, skipped, missing, external, path_policy_violation, ambiguous, and stale counts. Reason-level details such as skipped_cycle, skipped_depth, skipped_limit, skipped_file_count, skipped_total_bytes, excluded_target, non_markdown_target, or encoded_traversal are diagnostic_code values under the canonical status, not separate edge statuses.
- [ ] AC-8: Newly saved or updated documents are enqueued through the common post-save indexing entrypoint with requested_by=local.linked_import or equivalent value so metadata extraction, category/documentTags classification, persistent link graph, chunking, keyword FTS, optional embedding, HNSW update lifecycle, and background reconciliation can run without blocking the import result.
- [ ] AC-9: Import result, diagnostics, logs, backup/export manifest, Settings diagnostics, and smart_search trace expose documentId, imported documentIds, source-relative source path, redacted href or path token, status counts, and diagnostic codes, and do not expose raw local absolute path, canonical path, source root path, destination path, raw href, normalized href, or app userData index path.
- [ ] AC-10: E2E fixture covers A.md -> B.md -> C.md -> A.md, self-link, duplicate links, duplicate basename, already imported B.md, missing target, outside-root target, symlink/junction escape, URL-encoded spaces, encoded traversal variants, Korean filename, canonical source saved at most once, no infinite loop, canonical resolved/missing/skipped diagnostics, and search_documents plus smart_search retrieval/diagnostics.

### ~~CON-DOC-005 — Linked local Markdown import is duplicate-safe user-confirmed MCP-inert and canonical-diagnostic~~ [DISCARDED → see CON-DOC-007]

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | constraint |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | discarded |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, local-import, deduplication, source-identity, mcp-inert, path-policy, sqlite, redaction, concurrency, broken-links, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-linked-import-dedup/report.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-broken-link-policy/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 linked local Markdown import를 사용자가 명시적으로 승인한 Settings/local import workflow로만 실행해야 하며, 어떤 MCP 도구도 이 import나 reconciliation control을 직접 또는 암묵적으로 시작하지 않아야 한다. Importer는 DR-DOC-012의 source identity lookup, SQLite uniqueness/upsert, canonical edge status plus diagnostic_code, destination containment, path/href redaction, and encoded traversal rejection을 함께 적용해 이미 저장된 문서를 재저장하지 않고, 동시 실행이나 path alias가 있어도 하나의 canonical source에 하나의 active documentId와 저장본만 유지해야 한다.

#### Rationale

Supersedes CON-DOC-002 because that constraint still traced to discarded DR-DOC-011. This replacement preserves the duplicate-safe, user-confirmed, MCP-inert invariant while depending on DR-DOC-012 and CON-DOC-004 as the active source identity and broken-link policy requirements.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: Linked local Markdown import starts only from Settings/import dialog or a source-path-backed local import action after the user explicitly approves the import root and execution. open_markdown, update_markdown, save_document, search_documents, search_projects, smart_search, and any other MCP tool call must not directly or implicitly start linked import, recursive import, bulk source scan, indexing rebuild, clear, retry, cancel, model change, or broken-link reconciliation controls.
- [ ] AC-2: Importer captures the approved import root realpath or equivalent canonical identity plus root_fingerprint at approval time, and revalidates lexical containment and canonical realpath containment before each candidate read or copy. TOCTOU symlink, junction, hard-link escape, root replacement, or outside-root target produces path_policy_violation diagnostic and no document row or destination write.
- [ ] AC-3: Same-run traversal uses active recursion stack and visited set keyed by canonical realpath hash or canonical source identity. It does not decide cycle or duplicate status from raw absolute path, destination path, content_hash, inode/file-id, or documentId alone.
- [ ] AC-4: Persistent duplicate or update lookup uses SQLite unique constraint or equivalent transaction boundary on source_id plus path_key and canonical path hash alias. Concurrent import jobs for the same canonical source converge to one active documentId and one destination write; loser jobs report existing, skipped, or skipped_duplicate diagnostic_code under the canonical result/status contract.
- [ ] AC-5: If active source_id plus path_key or unique canonical path hash alias exists, importer reuses the existing documentId and does not write a new file. Unchanged content skips duplicate write and duplicate indexing job; changed content becomes a revision update on the same documentId and enqueues common post-save indexing.
- [ ] AC-6: Preserving documentId for a move candidate requires matching file-id, inode, or equivalent platform evidence, or explicit user approval when the previous path is inactive, missing, or deleted. content_hash alone is insufficient, and active previous paths remain copy/duplicate candidates rather than auto-merge targets.
- [ ] AC-7: Destination path is derived only after source identity is resolved. Absolute segment, drive/UNC segment, dot-dot traversal, empty/NUL-like segment, encoded slash/backslash/traversal, reserved device name, case or Unicode normalization collision, destination collision, and failed final destination realpath containment are rejected or isolated with deterministic conflict handling and redacted diagnostics.
- [ ] AC-8: If an existing destination file or legacy saved document lacks a source identity ledger row, importer may adopt it only when content_hash, frontmatter identity, optional file-id/inode, destination containment, and DR-DOC-012 identity evidence are all consistent. Otherwise it returns ambiguous diagnostic and does not overwrite or merge.
- [ ] AC-9: Local import result, diagnostics, logs, backup/export manifest, smart_search trace, and Settings diagnostics expose only documentId, source-relative path, redacted href/path token, canonical status counts, and diagnostic codes. They must not expose raw local absolute path, canonical path, app userData index path, source root path, destination path, raw href, normalized href, or provider credential URL.
- [ ] AC-10: Release-gating fixture covers root document already imported, linked child already imported, concurrent duplicate import, symlink/junction alias, case-insensitive alias, root fingerprint change, destination collision, legacy destination without source identity, percent-encoded traversal variants, double-encoded traversal, NUL-like input, Windows reserved names, Unicode normalization collision, Korean/space filename, canonical source saved at most once, and canonical diagnostics/redaction.

### ~~FR-DOC-032 — Existing knowledge-store Markdown files are adopted without rewrite and use canonical broken-link diagnostics~~ [DISCARDED → see FR-DOC-034]

| Risk | high |

| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-linked-import-dedup/report.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-broken-link-policy/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 configured knowledge-store root 안에 이미 존재하지만 MCP save_document, viewer save, local linked import로 등록된 source identity가 없는 Markdown 파일도 기존 파일을 다시 쓰거나 복사하지 않고 smart indexing 대상으로 채택해야 한다. 채택된 legacy document는 stable documentId, knowledge-store source identity, source-relative path_key, content hash, and DR-DOC-012 canonical link edge diagnostics를 SQLite 원장에 기록하고, broken link는 실패나 삭제가 아니라 CON-DOC-004에 따라 검색과 진단에 사용할 수 있는 non-blocking diagnostic edge로 보존해야 한다.

#### Rationale

Supersedes FR-DOC-030 because that requirement still traced to discarded DR-DOC-011. This replacement preserves legacy adoption and broken-link diagnostics behavior while depending on DR-DOC-012, CON-DOC-005, and CON-DOC-004 as the active source identity, duplicate-safety, and broken-link lifecycle requirements.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] AC-1: Settings indexing startup, source-root scan, or equivalent background indexing path can adopt .md and .markdown files inside the configured knowledge-store root that lack source identity/import ledger rows as legacy knowledge-store documents. This action is not started by MCP search calls or any MCP import/indexing control tool.
- [ ] AC-2: Legacy adoption does not rewrite, copy, move, or rename existing files. It records source_kind=knowledge_store or equivalent marker, stable documentId, source-relative path, normalized path_key, content_hash, mtime/size or equivalent freshness evidence, and path_status=active in SQLite documents/source identity ledger.
- [ ] AC-3: If a legacy file contains trusted DocuLight documentId frontmatter or equivalent stable id and it passes SQLite uniqueness/redaction policy, the id may be reused. If absent or conflicting, the ledger insert generates a stable documentId without modifying file content.
- [ ] AC-4: Legacy adoption does not automatically merge with local source-path import identity. Same content_hash, basename, title, or docName alone is insufficient to treat a linked import source as the same document; merge, overwrite, or destination replacement requires CON-DOC-005 evidence or explicit user approval.
- [ ] AC-5: Legacy adopted document is enqueued with requested_by=knowledge_store.legacy_adoption or equivalent value through the common post-save indexing entrypoint for metadata extraction, category/documentTags classification, persistent link graph, chunking, keyword FTS, optional embedding, HNSW update lifecycle, and background link reconciliation. Unchanged rescans refresh freshness without duplicate writes or duplicate indexing jobs.
- [ ] AC-6: Legacy link extraction uses parser-compatible Markdown inline links and wikilinks, and target resolution stays within the same knowledge-store source root and approved source records. If target resolves to a unique active documentId/path_key, edge status is resolved; otherwise the edge uses DR-DOC-012 canonical status plus diagnostic_code and creates no document row for missing, external, skipped, path_policy_violation, ambiguous, or stale outcomes.
- [ ] AC-7: Broken link edge is not an indexing failure. It preserves internal original href, normalized href, target anchor, source documentId, source chunk/line or equivalent location, canonical edge status, and diagnostic_code in the ledger. Only resolved edges can affect resolved-link ranking, while broken/unresolved counts may appear in includeDiagnostics or Settings diagnostics after redaction.
- [ ] AC-8: If a missing, ambiguous, or stale target document is later saved, legacy-adopted, or local-imported, the next normal background indexing/link graph reconciliation can update the edge to resolved. If a target document is deleted, excluded, tombstoned, or moved without confirmed identity, edge status becomes stale or missing without failing source document indexing.
- [ ] AC-9: Legacy adoption and broken-link diagnostics external responses, logs, backup/export manifest, smart_search trace, and Settings diagnostics expose only documentId, source-relative path, redacted href/path token, canonical edge status counts, and diagnostic code. They do not expose raw local absolute path, canonical path, knowledge-store root path, app userData path, destination path, raw href, or normalized href.
- [ ] AC-10: Release-gating fixture covers pre-existing knowledge-store root files without ledger rows, trusted documentId frontmatter reuse, documentId collision, duplicate content in two active files, same basename in different directories, broken relative link, broken wikilink, target added later, target removed later, excluded target, outside-root link, symlink/junction escape, encoded traversal variant, Korean/space filename, no rewrite, stable documentId, indexing enqueue, canonical missing/resolved/stale reconciliation, and redaction.

### DR-DOC-013 — Local source identity ledger uses active canonical link edge diagnostics

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | data |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, local-import, source-identity, deduplication, sqlite, link-graph, broken-links, path-policy, redaction, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-broken-link-policy/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 로컬 source-path import와 linked-document import가 같은 원본 문서를 여러 번 저장하지 않도록 source identity와 import graph 원장을 SQLite에 유지해야 하며, link edge 상태는 active canonical lifecycle enum과 diagnostic_code 조합으로 저장해야 한다. 원장은 stable documentId, source root fingerprint, source-relative path key, canonical realpath hash, content hash, import job state, canonical edge status, diagnostic_code를 분리해 저장하고 raw absolute path와 raw href는 internal-only 값으로 처리해야 한다.

#### Rationale

Supersedes DR-DOC-012 because final trace evaluation found that DR-DOC-012 still had depends_on links to discarded requirements. This replacement preserves the canonical ledger model and uses only active dependencies plus a supersedes relation to the prior requirement.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: SQLite schema represents local import source identity with source_id, root_fingerprint, source_relative_path, path_key, canonical_path_internal or canonical_path_hash, content_hash, documentId, source_mtime_or_revision, first_seen_at, last_seen_at, last_import_job_id, and import_state. Raw absolute path and canonical path source values are internal-only fields.
- [x] AC-2: Primary document identity is stable documentId, and local source duplicate or update lookup uses source_id plus path_key together with canonical path hash alias. content_hash is used for no-op update, stale job, vector reuse, and move or copy candidate handling, but is not the primary identity by itself.
- [x] AC-3: Import traversal visited set uses canonical realpath hash or canonical source identity to block same-run cycles and symlink alias loops. Persistent duplicate check uses source root fingerprint and source-relative path key to prevent repeated saves across runs.
- [x] AC-4: If the same active source_id plus path_key already exists, importer reuses the existing documentId. If content hash is unchanged, duplicate write and duplicate indexing job are skipped; if content hash changed, the same documentId receives a revision update and common post-save indexing enqueue.
- [x] AC-5: If a new path appears while a previous path is inactive, missing, or deleted, importer may preserve documentId only with matching file-id, inode, or equivalent platform evidence, or explicit user approval. If the previous path remains active, identical content is recorded as copy or duplicate candidate and is not auto-merged.
- [x] AC-6: Import graph ledger tracks root document, parent source identity, target source identity, depth, original href, normalized href, anchor, canonical edge status, diagnostic_code, and linked documentId when resolved. The canonical edge status enum is exactly resolved, missing, external, path_policy_violation, skipped, ambiguous, stale; cycle, depth limit, file count limit, byte limit, excluded target, non-Markdown target, image or binary target, encoded traversal, and containment failure are diagnostic_code values under the appropriate canonical status.
- [x] AC-7: Path policy failures, missing files, excluded files, external links, skipped traversal limits, ambiguous candidates, and stale targets are retained as edge diagnostics for graph/search diagnostics but do not create document rows, source identity rows, destination files, or duplicate files in the knowledge-store root.
- [x] AC-8: All external responses, logs, diagnostics export, backup manifest, Settings diagnostics, smart_search diagnostics, and smart_search traces expose only documentId, source-relative path, redacted href or path token, status counts, and diagnostic codes. They must not expose raw local absolute path, canonical path, raw href, normalized href, app userData index path, or credential-bearing provider URL.
- [x] AC-9: Schema and reconciliation fixtures verify Windows drive paths, UNC paths, POSIX paths, case-insensitive collision, Unicode normalization collision, symlink or junction alias, root fingerprint change, duplicate basename, identical content in different active paths, moved path candidate, broken relative link, broken wikilink, skipped cycle, skipped depth, target added later, target removed later, and Korean or space path normalization.

### CON-DOC-006 — Broken Markdown link lifecycle uses active ledger diagnostics and MCP-inert background reconciliation

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | constraint |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, link-graph, broken-links, diagnostics, redaction, mcp-inert, smart-search, background-indexing, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-broken-link-policy/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 linked local import, legacy knowledge-store adoption, persistent link graph indexing에서 깨진 Markdown 링크를 인덱싱 실패나 자동 import 신호로 처리하지 않고, DR-DOC-013의 active canonical edge status와 redacted diagnostics를 가진 non-blocking graph edge로 관리해야 한다. resolved edge만 link ranking과 linkedTo/linkedFrom 필터에 사용하고, broken edge는 문서 저장, source identity 생성, destination write, recursive import, MCP-triggered synchronous reconciliation 또는 MCP control surface를 만들지 않아야 한다. 일반 save/adoption/import 후 background indexing reconciliation은 별도 작업으로만 상태를 갱신할 수 있다.

#### Rationale

Supersedes CON-DOC-004 because final trace evaluation found active links from CON-DOC-004 to discarded local import requirements. This replacement keeps the same broken-link lifecycle policy while depending on the active ledger DR-DOC-013 and active replacement requirements only.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: Broken-link policy applies uniformly to linked local source-path import, legacy knowledge-store adoption, persistent link graph indexing, Settings diagnostics, export/backup manifests, logs, smart_search diagnostics, and smart_search traces.
- [x] AC-2: Persistent link graph stores each extracted Markdown inline link or wikilink edge with the canonical status enum from DR-DOC-013: resolved, missing, external, path_policy_violation, skipped, ambiguous, stale. Reason-level details such as cycle, depth limit, excluded target, non-Markdown target, encoded traversal, or containment failure are diagnostic_code values, not separate edge statuses.
- [x] AC-3: resolved status requires a unique active target documentId or path_key. Only resolved edges may set to_document_id, contribute linkScore, reciprocal link, or same-cluster boost, or satisfy linkedTo and linkedFrom filters.
- [x] AC-4: missing, external, skipped, ambiguous, and stale edges are non-blocking diagnostics. They must not create document rows, source identity rows, destination files, recursive import jobs, or resolved-link ranking boost, and they must not cause the source document indexing job to fail when the source document itself is readable.
- [x] AC-5: path_policy_violation is used for outside-root targets, lexical traversal, encoded slash or backslash traversal, NUL-like input, drive, UNC, or absolute path segments, root replacement, symlink or junction escape, or failed canonical realpath containment. Such edges must not create document rows, destination writes, source identity rows, or import jobs.
- [x] AC-6: Target resolution performs lexical containment before filesystem probing and canonical realpath containment before read, copy, or write. If resolution yields multiple active candidates by documentId, path_key, docName, basename, case folding, or Unicode normalization, the edge status is ambiguous and the system must not auto-merge, overwrite, or pick a winner without unique stable identity evidence.
- [x] AC-7: raw href, normalized href, canonical path, source root, destination path, app userData path, and credential-bearing provider values are internal-only. External responses, Settings diagnostics, logs, exports, backup manifests, and smart_search traces may expose only documentId, source-relative source path, redacted href or path token, edge status, diagnostic_code, and aggregate counts.
- [x] AC-8: BM25, FTS, and smart_search keyword text must not be polluted with synthetic diagnostic tokens such as broken or missing. Authored link text or href may remain searchable as normal document content, but broken-edge metadata is exposed only through link graph diagnostics.
- [x] AC-9: Reconciliation transitions missing, ambiguous, or stale edges to resolved only when a later save_document, viewer save, legacy adoption, or local import has produced a unique active target and the normal background indexing or link graph reconciliation job processes that target. Target deletion, exclusion, tombstone, or unconfirmed move transitions a previously resolved edge to stale or missing without failing source document indexing.
- [x] AC-10: MCP open_markdown, update_markdown, save_document, search_documents, search_projects, and smart_search must not treat Markdown links or MCP-supplied path-like fields as approved import roots and must not expose or synchronously trigger recursive import, bulk scan, rebuild, retry, cancel, or broken-link reconciliation controls. save_document may write a document and enqueue ordinary post-save indexing, but any broken-link resolution happens later through the background indexing lifecycle and is only observable as current redacted diagnostics.
- [x] AC-11: smart_search includeDiagnostics may expose redacted aggregate link status counts, link graph readiness, and last reconciliation or index revision. linkedTo and linkedFrom remain resolved-document filters only and must not match missing, external, skipped, ambiguous, stale, or path_policy_violation edges.
- [x] AC-12: Release-gating fixtures cover broken relative link, broken wikilink, external URL, anchor-only link, excluded target, non-Markdown target, outside-root relative link, Windows absolute path, UNC path, POSIX absolute path, symlink or junction escape, encoded traversal variants, ambiguous basename, case, and Unicode collision, target added later, target removed later, Korean and space filename, smart_search includeDiagnostics redaction, absence of indexing failure for readable source documents, and absence of MCP import/reconciliation controls.

Treat save_document as a write plus ordinary indexing enqueue only; do not add MCP reconciliation controls.

### FR-DOC-033 — Local linked Markdown import preserves graph connectivity with active canonical diagnostics

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | functional |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, local-import, linked-documents, knowledge-store, link-graph, deduplication, path-policy, settings-import, broken-links, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-broken-link-policy/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 source-path가 있는 로컬 Markdown 문서를 사용자가 지식 저장소로 가져오도록 명시적으로 실행하면, 해당 문서의 로컬 Markdown 링크와 링크된 문서의 링크를 bounded traversal로 따라가며 지식 저장소에 저장해야 한다. 재귀 import는 무한 루프를 방지하고 이미 저장된 source 문서를 중복 저장하지 않으며, 모든 unresolved 또는 skipped link outcome은 DR-DOC-013의 canonical edge status와 diagnostic_code로 기록되어 background indexing과 smart_search diagnostics에서 사용할 수 있어야 한다.

#### Rationale

Supersedes FR-DOC-031 because DR-DOC-012 and CON-DOC-004 were superseded to remove stale trace dependencies. This replacement keeps linked import behavior and depends only on active canonical diagnostic requirements.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: Linked-document import runs only when the user explicitly starts a source-path-backed local Markdown import or Settings/import dialog workflow. It must not add filePath, sourceFilePath, recursive, forceIndex, rebuild, or equivalent import/indexing control fields to the content-only save_document MCP schema, and it must not add a separate MCP indexing/import control tool.
- [x] AC-2: Importer deterministically resolves Markdown inline links and wikilinks relative to the root document directory and treats only .md and .markdown local targets as import candidates. Remote URL, protocol URL, anchor-only link, image, binary, and non-Markdown targets are not imported and are recorded as link edge diagnostics using DR-DOC-013 canonical status plus diagnostic_code.
- [x] AC-3: Traversal uses deterministic order, configurable default depth at least 2, max depth, max file count, max total bytes, AbortSignal or equivalent cancel boundary, and a visited canonical source identity set so cycles, self-links, duplicated edges, symlink aliases, and repeated candidates terminate without infinite loops.
- [x] AC-4: Each import candidate is lexically normalized and, when filesystem probing is needed, canonical realpath containment is checked against the configured source root or user-approved import root. Traversal, symlink, junction, hard-link escape, outside-root target, encoded slash/backslash traversal, absolute drive/UNC/POSIX segment, and root replacement produce path_policy_violation diagnostics and no document row or destination write.
- [x] AC-5: If a stored source identity already exists, importer reuses the existing documentId and does not write a new file. If content hash is unchanged, duplicate write and duplicate indexing job are skipped; if content hash changed, the same documentId receives a revision update and common post-save indexing enqueue.
- [x] AC-6: Newly saved linked documents stay under the configured knowledge-store root using source-relative layout or deterministic source-to-destination map. If link rewrite is performed, the ledger tracks original href, normalized target, rewritten href, canonical edge status, diagnostic_code, and target documentId when resolved.
- [x] AC-7: Import result reports imported, updated, existing, skipped, missing, external, path_policy_violation, ambiguous, and stale counts. Reason-level details such as skipped_cycle, skipped_depth, skipped_limit, skipped_file_count, skipped_total_bytes, excluded_target, non_markdown_target, or encoded_traversal are diagnostic_code values under canonical status, not separate edge statuses.
- [x] AC-8: Newly saved or updated documents are enqueued through the common post-save indexing entrypoint with requested_by=local.linked_import or equivalent value so metadata extraction, classification, persistent link graph, chunking, FTS, optional embedding, HNSW lifecycle, and background reconciliation can run without blocking the import result.
- [x] AC-9: Import result, diagnostics, logs, backup/export manifest, Settings diagnostics, and smart_search trace expose documentId, imported documentIds, source-relative source path, redacted href/path token, status counts, and diagnostic codes, and do not expose raw local absolute path, canonical path, source root path, destination path, raw href, normalized href, or app userData index path.
- [x] AC-10: E2E fixture covers A.md -> B.md -> C.md -> A.md, self-link, duplicate links, duplicate basename, already imported child, missing target, outside-root target, symlink/junction escape, encoded traversal variants, Korean filename, canonical source saved at most once, no infinite loop, canonical resolved/missing/skipped diagnostics, and search_documents plus smart_search retrieval/diagnostics.

### CON-DOC-007 — Linked local Markdown import is duplicate-safe user-confirmed MCP-inert with active canonical diagnostics

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Type | constraint |
| Target | 0.11.0-w2 |
| Status | verified |
| Priority | critical |
| Tags | wave-2, local-import, deduplication, source-identity, mcp-inert, path-policy, sqlite, redaction, concurrency, broken-links, feasibility:high |
| Risk | high |
| Stability | stable |
| Verification Method | test |
| GitHub Issue | - |
| Related Docs | docs/research/knowledge_store/knowledge.md, docs/analysis/kiwi-srs-2026-07-01.doculight-broken-link-policy/report.md |

#### Requirement

시스템은 linked local Markdown import를 사용자가 명시적으로 승인한 Settings/local import workflow로만 실행해야 하며, 어떤 MCP 도구도 이 import나 reconciliation control을 직접 또는 암묵적으로 시작하지 않아야 한다. Importer는 DR-DOC-013의 source identity lookup, SQLite uniqueness/upsert, canonical edge status plus diagnostic_code, destination containment, path/href redaction, and encoded traversal rejection을 함께 적용해 이미 저장된 문서를 재저장하지 않고, 동시 실행이나 path alias가 있어도 하나의 canonical source에 하나의 active documentId와 저장본만 유지해야 한다.

#### Rationale

Supersedes CON-DOC-005 because DR-DOC-012 and CON-DOC-004 were superseded to remove stale trace dependencies. This replacement keeps duplicate-safe, user-confirmed, MCP-inert behavior and depends only on active canonical diagnostic requirements.

#### Acceptance Criteria

- [x] AC-1: Linked local Markdown import starts only from Settings/import dialog or a source-path-backed local import action after the user explicitly approves the import root and execution. MCP tools must not directly or implicitly start linked import, recursive import, bulk source scan, indexing rebuild, clear, retry, cancel, model change, or broken-link reconciliation controls.
- [x] AC-2: Importer captures the approved import root realpath or equivalent canonical identity plus root_fingerprint at approval time, and revalidates lexical containment and canonical realpath containment before each candidate read or copy. TOCTOU symlink, junction, hard-link escape, root replacement, or outside-root target produces path_policy_violation diagnostic and no document row or destination write.
- [x] AC-3: Same-run traversal uses active recursion stack and visited set keyed by canonical realpath hash or canonical source identity. It does not decide cycle or duplicate status from raw absolute path, destination path, content_hash, inode/file-id, or documentId alone.
- [x] AC-4: Persistent duplicate or update lookup uses SQLite unique constraint or equivalent transaction boundary on source_id plus path_key and canonical path hash alias. Concurrent import jobs for the same canonical source converge to one active documentId and one destination write; loser jobs report existing, skipped, or skipped_duplicate diagnostic_code under canonical result/status contract.
- [x] AC-5: If active source_id plus path_key or unique canonical path hash alias exists, importer reuses the existing documentId and does not write a new file. Unchanged content skips duplicate write and duplicate indexing job; changed content becomes a revision update on the same documentId and enqueues common post-save indexing.
- [x] AC-6: Preserving documentId for a move candidate requires matching file-id, inode, or equivalent platform evidence, or explicit user approval when the previous path is inactive, missing, or deleted. content_hash alone is insufficient, and active previous paths remain copy/duplicate candidates rather than auto-merge targets.
- [x] AC-7: Destination path is derived only after source identity is resolved. Absolute segment, drive/UNC segment, dot-dot traversal, empty/NUL-like segment, encoded slash/backslash/traversal, reserved device name, case or Unicode normalization collision, destination collision, and failed final destination realpath containment are rejected or isolated with deterministic conflict handling and redacted diagnostics.
- [x] AC-8: If an existing destination file or legacy saved document lacks a source identity ledger row, importer may adopt it only when content_hash, frontmatter identity, optional file-id/inode, destination containment, and DR-DOC-013 identity evidence are all consistent. Otherwise it returns ambiguous diagnostic and does not overwrite or merge.
- [x] AC-9: Local import result, diagnostics, logs, backup/export manifest, smart_search trace, and Settings diagnostics expose only documentId, source-relative path, redacted href/path token, canonical status counts, and diagnostic codes. They must not expose raw local absolute path, canonical path, app userData index path, source root path, destination path, raw href, normalized href, or provider credential URL.
- [x] AC-10: Release-gating fixture covers root document already imported, linked child already imported, concurrent duplicate import, symlink/junction alias, case-insensitive alias, root fingerprint change, destination collision, legacy destination without source identity, encoded traversal variants, NUL-like input, reserved names, Unicode normalization collision, Korean/space filename, canonical source saved at most once, and canonical diagnostics/redaction.

- Final evaluator required active requirements to avoid dependencies on superseded requirements. This replacement points duplicate-safety constraints to DR-DOC-013, CON-DOC-006, and FR-DOC-033.

#### Implementation Notes

- Implement as an internal Settings/local import service layered over DR-DOC-013. Absolute paths and hrefs are internal inputs only. Use transactionally enforced unique source identity rows and diagnostic codes for skipped/broken outcomes.
